# Detail CRM — database schema (column-level contract)

> Generated by `python3 scripts/gen_types.py` from a throwaway Postgres cluster built with `scripts/test_db.sh` (local Supabase shim + every migration from zero). **Do not edit by hand**: change a migration and regenerate. `python3 scripts/check_contracts.py` fails when this file or `web/src/lib/database.types.ts` is stale, and verifies every table, column, RPC and storage bucket that the web app, the iOS app and the edge functions reference against the same live schema (SPEC §8.2).

- Migrations applied: 38 (`0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql` … `0093_integration_money.sql`), content sha256/16: `0ba8e2d3443a08df`
- Counts: 54 tables, 0 views, 316 functions (204 non-trigger), 33 enums, 1 composite types, 154 public RLS policies, 3 storage buckets (13 storage.objects policies)
- TypeScript: `web/src/lib/database.types.ts` (same generator run). Swift: models map these names 1:1 through explicit `CodingKeys` under a `// table: <name>` or `// rpc: <name>` annotation.
- Legend: grants `S/I/U/D` = table-level SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE for the PostgREST role (RLS still applies; `service_role` bypasses RLS). `cols UPDATE(a, b)` = column-level grant only. Policy expressions are abbreviated (`public.` stripped, whitespace collapsed, long ones truncated with …). `exec:` = roles with EXECUTE on a function (includes PUBLIC grants). DEFINER = SECURITY DEFINER.
- Called by: the web (`web/src`), iOS (`ios/`) and edge-function (`supabase/functions/<name>`) files that call the function by a static name (the same scan `scripts/check_contracts.py` verifies; tests excluded), pg_cron jobs and other setup SQL in `supabase/setup/`, other public SQL functions (`public.<fn>(` in their body) and RLS policies (public tables and `storage.objects`). Trigger functions list their triggers instead.
- Deviations from `supabase gen types typescript` (opt-in per object, through contract tags in the migrations; everything else is byte-identical to the Supabase generator): a column commented `@insert-optional` — a NOT NULL column without a default that a BEFORE INSERT trigger always fills — is optional in its table's `Insert` type (Supabase makes it required); a function commented `@nullable: a, b` types those RETURNS TABLE output columns `T | null` (Supabase types every RETURNS TABLE column non-null, because Postgres does not record their nullability). Such columns are listed per table / function below.

## Foundation (0001–0009): tenancy, shop setup, CRM, catalog, jobs, scheduling

### Enums

- `business_type`: fixed | mobile | both  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `coupon_kind`: percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `customer_lifecycle`: lead | customer  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `customer_source`: staff | online_booking | referral | google | facebook | instagram | walk_in | other  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `deposit_type`: percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `discount_kind`: none | percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `document_kind`: job | quote | invoice  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `job_source`: staff | online_booking | quote | membership  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `job_status`: requested | scheduled | confirmed | en_route | in_progress | completed | cancelled | no_show  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `location_type`: shop | mobile  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `resource_kind`: bay | van | other  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `service_kind`: service | package | addon | product  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `shop_role`: owner | admin | manager | technician  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_

### Composite types

- `document_totals`(subtotal_cents bigint, discount_cents bigint, taxable_subtotal_cents bigint, taxable_discount_cents bigint, tax_cents bigint, total_cents bigint)  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_ — Result of compute_document_totals (shared by jobs, quotes, invoices).

### Tables

#### `blocked_times`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> blocked_times — member_id null blocks the whole shop (and online booking).

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| member_id | uuid | yes |  |
| starts_at | timestamp with time zone | no |  |
| ends_at | timestamp with time zone | no |  |
| reason | text | yes |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `blocked_times_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `blocked_times_member_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, member_id) REFERENCES shop_members(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `blocked_times_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `blocked_times_order` (ends_at > starts_at); `blocked_times_reason_check` (reason IS NULL OR char_length(reason) <= 500)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `blocked_times_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `blocked_times_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `blocked_times_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) OR (is_shop_member(shop_id) AND ((member_id IS NULL) OR is_own_member(member_id))))`
  - `blocked_times_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `blocked_times_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `blocked_times_20_set_created_by` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → set_created_by(); `blocked_times_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `booking_settings`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> booking_settings — one row per shop (seeded, disabled by default).

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| enabled | boolean | no | false |
| auto_confirm | boolean | no | false |
| lead_time_minutes | integer | no | 120 |
| max_days_ahead | integer | no | 60 |
| slot_interval_minutes | integer | no | 30 |
| buffer_minutes | integer | no | 0 |
| max_concurrent_jobs | integer | no | 1 |
| require_deposit | boolean | no | false |
| deposit_type | deposit_type | no | 'percent'::deposit_type |
| deposit_value | bigint | no | 0 |
| service_area_postal_codes | text[] | no | '{}'::text[] |
| booking_message | text | yes |  |
| cancellation_policy | text | yes |  |
| allow_client_cancel_hours | integer | no | 24 |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (shop_id)
- FK `booking_settings_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `booking_settings_allow_client_cancel_hours_check` (allow_client_cancel_hours >= 0 AND allow_client_cancel_hours <= 8760); `booking_settings_booking_message_check` (booking_message IS NULL OR char_length(booking_message) <= 5000); `booking_settings_buffer_minutes_check` (buffer_minutes >= 0 AND buffer_minutes <= 480); `booking_settings_cancellation_policy_check` (cancellation_policy IS NULL OR char_length(cancellation_policy) <= 5000); `booking_settings_deposit_percent` (deposit_type <> 'percent'::deposit_type OR deposit_value <= 10000); `booking_settings_deposit_required` (NOT require_deposit OR deposit_value > 0); `booking_settings_deposit_value_check` (deposit_value >= 0); `booking_settings_lead_time_minutes_check` (lead_time_minutes >= 0 AND lead_time_minutes <= 43200); `booking_settings_max_concurrent_jobs_check` (max_concurrent_jobs >= 1 AND max_concurrent_jobs <= 100); `booking_settings_max_days_ahead_check` (max_days_ahead >= 1 AND max_days_ahead <= 365); `booking_settings_service_area_postal_codes_check` (array_position(service_area_postal_codes, NULL::text) IS NULL); `booking_settings_slot_interval_minutes_check` (slot_interval_minutes >= 5 AND slot_interval_minutes <= 240)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SU
- Policies:
  - `booking_settings_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_member(shop_id)`
  - `booking_settings_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `booking_settings_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `booking_settings_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `business_hours`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> business_hours — weekday 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday (extract(dow)). Several non-overlapping intervals per day allowed; no row = closed. Times are wall clock in the shop's time zone; closes_at may be 24:00.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |

- CHECK: `shops_address_line1_check` (address_line1 IS NULL OR char_length(address_line1) <= 200); `shops_address_line2_check` (address_line2 IS NULL OR char_length(address_line2) <= 200); `shops_brand_color_check` (brand_color IS NULL OR is_valid_hex_color(brand_color)); `shops_city_check` (city IS NULL OR char_length(city) <= 100); `shops_country_check` (country ~ '^[A-Z]{2}$'); `shops_currency_check` (currency ~ '^[a-z]{3}$'); `shops_email_check` (email IS NULL OR is_valid_email(email::text)); `shops_invoice_due_days_check` (invoice_due_days >= 0 AND invoice_due_days <= 365); `shops_invoice_terms_check` (invoice_terms IS NULL OR char_length(invoice_terms) <= 20000); `shops_lat_check` (lat IS NULL OR lat >= '-90'::integer::double precision AND lat <= 90::double precision); `shops_lat_lng_pair` ((lat IS NULL) = (lng IS NULL)); `shops_lng_check` (lng IS NULL OR lng >= '-180'::integer::double precision AND lng <= 180::double precision); `shops_logo_path_check` (logo_path IS NULL OR is_shop_asset_path(id, logo_path)); `shops_name_check` (char_length(btrim(name)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(name)) <= 120); `shops_phone_check` (phone IS NULL OR is_valid_e164(phone)); `shops_postal_code_check` (postal_code IS NULL OR char_length(postal_code) <= 20); `shops_quote_terms_check` (quote_terms IS NULL OR char_length(quote_terms) <= 20000); `shops_region_check` (region IS NULL OR char_length(region) <= 100); `shops_review_url_check` (review_url IS NULL OR char_length(review_url) <= 1000); `shops_slug_check` (is_valid_slug(slug)); `shops_sms_from_number_check` (sms_from_number IS NULL OR is_valid_e164(sms_from_number)); `shops_tax_rate_bps_check` (tax_rate_bps >= 0 AND tax_rate_bps <= 10000); `shops_website_check` (website IS NULL OR char_length(website) <= 500)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SUD
- Policies:
  - `shops_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `has_shop_role(id, VARIADIC ARRAY['owner'::shop_role])`
  - `shops_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_member(id)`
  - `shops_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(id)`
- Triggers: `shops_10_before_write` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → shops_before_write(); `shops_20_money_delete_guard` BEFORE DELETE ROW → shops_money_delete_guard(); `shops_30_sms_from_number_bound` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF sms_from_number ROW → shops_sms_from_number_bound(); `shops_90_remember_name_on_delete` BEFORE DELETE ROW → shops_remember_name_on_delete(); `shops_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `shops_logo_object_exists` AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF logo_path ROW → require_shop_asset_object('logo_path'); `shops_queue_storage_purge` AFTER DELETE ROW → shops_queue_storage_purge(); `shops_require_owner` AFTER INSERT DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED ROW → shops_require_owner(); `shops_seed_comms` AFTER INSERT ROW → shops_seed_comms(); `shops_seed_shop_setup` AFTER INSERT ROW → shops_seed_shop_setup(); `shops_seed_tenancy` AFTER INSERT ROW → shops_seed_tenancy()

#### `vehicles`

file `0004_foundation_crm.sql` · also altered/policies in `0006_foundation_jobs.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |

| year | smallint | yes |  |
| make | text | yes |  |
| model | text | yes |  |
| trim | text | yes |  |
| color | text | yes |  |
| vin | text | yes |  |
| license_plate | text | yes |  |
| category_id | uuid | yes |  |
| notes | text | yes |  |
| archived_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| search_text | text | yes | GENERATED: lower(((((((((((((COALESCE((year)::text, '') \|\| ' ') \|\| COALESCE(make, '')) \|\| ' ') \|\| COALESCE(model, '')) \|\| ' ') \|\| COALESCE("trim", '')… |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)

- FK `vehicles_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `vehicles_color_check` (color IS NULL OR char_length(color) <= 40); `vehicles_license_plate_check` (license_plate IS NULL OR char_length(license_plate) <= 15); `vehicles_make_check` (make IS NULL OR char_length(make) <= 60); `vehicles_model_check` (model IS NULL OR char_length(model) <= 60); `vehicles_notes_check` (notes IS NULL OR char_length(notes) <= 20000); `vehicles_trim_check` ("trim" IS NULL OR char_length("trim") <= 60); `vehicles_vin_check` (vin IS NULL OR vin ~ '^[A-Z0-9]{5,17}$'); `vehicles_year_check` (year IS NULL OR year >= 1886 AND year <= 2100)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:

  - `vehicles_select_staff` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `vehicles_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `vehicles_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `vehicles_15_lock_owner_change` BEFORE UPDATE OF customer_id ROW WHEN ((new.customer_id IS DISTINCT FROM old.customer_id)) → vehicles_lock_owner_change(); `vehicles_20_normalize` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → vehicles_normalize(); `vehicles_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `vehicles_keep_owner_history` AFTER UPDATE OF customer_id ROW WHEN ((new.customer_id IS DISTINCT FROM old.customer_id)) → vehicles_keep_owner_history()

## Money (0010–0019): quotes, invoices, payments, cards, memberships, Stripe

### Enums

- `invoice_status`: draft | open | partially_paid | paid | void  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `membership_interval`: month | year  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `membership_status`: incomplete | active | past_due | cancelled  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_kind`: deposit | payment | membership  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_method`: card | card_present | cash | check | bank_transfer | other  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_status`: pending | succeeded | failed | cancelled | refunded | partially_refunded  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `quote_status`: draft | sent | viewed | approved | declined | expired | converted  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_

### Tables

#### `customer_payment_methods`

file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|

| brand | text | yes |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (public_token); (shop_id, id); (shop_id, number); invoices_one_per_job_key (shop_id, job_id) WHERE ((job_id IS NOT NULL) AND (status <> 'void'::invoice_status))
- FK `invoices_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `invoices_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
- FK `invoices_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE RESTRICT

#### `memberships`

file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql` · also altered/policies in `0041_integration_events.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> memberships Status machine: incomplete -> active | past_due | cancelled; active <-> past_due; active | past_due -> cancelled; cancelled is terminal. Staff may only change the vehicle directly; status is Stripe-driven (service_role). Even an incomplete membership may have a live Checkout link in subscription mode (membership_checkout) that the database does not know about, and paying it after the row was deleted or cancelled would start a subscription that bills with no active membership. So staff never delete memberships (they are history) and abandon an incomplete one only through the payments edge function's membership_cancel, which expires its open links (and stops a subscription a link…

| error | text | yes |  |

- PK: (id)
- CHECK: `stripe_events_account_check` (account IS NULL OR account ~ '^acct_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `stripe_events_attempts_check` (attempts >= 1); `stripe_events_error_check` (error IS NULL OR char_length(error) <= 5000); `stripe_events_id_check` (id ~ '^evt_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `stripe_events_type_check` (char_length(type) >= 1 AND char_length(type) <= 100)

### Functions

#### `apply_payment_to_invoice`

`apply_payment_to_invoice(p_payment_id uuid, p_invoice_id uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `payments`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`
- apply_payment_to_invoice(payment, invoice) — manager+. Puts received money that is not paying anything onto one of the same customer's issued invoices, so the customer never owes it twice: * an unapplied payment: no invoice, job or membership. Money received for a void invoice without a job, for a job / invoice / membership that was deleted, or for a document that moved to another customer is kept on the paying customer like this, with a note asking staff to apply it or refund it (payments_before_write, stripe-webhook); * an overpayment: a payment on another invoice that stays settled without it (its balance + the payment's net amount ≤ 0), e.g. a second payment link paid after the first one. The whole payment row moves, so its Stripe intent, refunds and tip stay together; its net amount (tips never count) must fit the target's balance less the card payments in flight on it (no new overpayment). The target must be open or partially paid. A job deposit still waiting for its job's invoice is not unapplied (create_invoice_from_job attaches it), and a membership payment belongs to its membership. A moved deposit becomes a plain payment (it now pays an invoice, not a job's deposit); th…
- Called by: no statically named web / iOS / edge-function / SQL caller in this repo

#### `coupon_redeem_for_job`

`coupon_redeem_for_job(p_shop_id uuid, p_coupon_id uuid)` → `coupons`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0012_money_invoices_payments.sql`
- jobs: coupons (SPEC §4.3 / §4.5 — the document discount is "coupon or manual"). A staff write (client context) that attaches a coupon redeems it: the coupon must be active, inside its window, under max_redemptions and not online_only; redemptions is incremented under the coupon's row lock and the job's discount is derived from the coupon (jobs_40_compute_totals then applies it). Removing or replacing the coupon releases its redemption and, unless a manual discount is set in the same write, removes its discount. While a coupon is attached its discount cannot be edited by hand. Once the job has a non-void invoice its coupon is frozen (the discount was billed). Trusted code manages coupons itself (create_online_booking redeems and sets the discount; ON DELETE SET NULL of a deleted coupon keeps the discount the job already received). The helpers run as the owner (coupon writes are admin-only under RLS) and refuse to run outside a trigger, so they cannot be called as RPCs.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `jobs_apply_coupon`

#### `invoice_link_token`

`invoice_link_token(p_invoice_id uuid)` → `uuid`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated · search_path="" · file `0015_money_privileges.sql`
- invoices.public_token is the customer's credential: whoever holds it can, without signing in, read the invoice (customer name, lines, payments with card brand/last4) and open Checkout for it — i.e. act as the customer. A technician who may collect payments on an assigned job reads that job's invoice (invoices_select) but must not keep a working customer link after the owner turns collecting off or removes them; technicians collect through record_manual_payment and the in-app PaymentSheet instead. So, as for jobs.public_token (0042) and form_submissions.public_token (0023): `authenticated` gets SELECT on every invoices column EXCEPT public_token, and owners/admins/managers fetch it with invoice_link_token(invoice_id) to share the /i link. service_role keeps full access (edge functions resolve tokens); anon has no table access. A migration that adds an invoices column must grant SELECT on it to authenticated (20_form_token_privacy.sql checks the whole column set).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/InvoiceService.swift`

Out-of-order safe: a cancelled membership never revives and an active/past_due one never regresses to incomplete (those updates only refresh the period fields). Idempotent. Billing terms: p_price_cents / p_interval / p_interval_count (all three or none) and p_price_id are the subscription's actual Stripe price (its unit amount × quantity); they replace the membership's recorded terms. Omitted, the recorded terms stay (the plan's terms captured while unlinked).

Idempotent and safe against out-of-order events: * linkage (invoice / job / customer / membership / kind / method) is fixed by the first call; later calls cannot move the money * money already received (succeeded / refunded states) is never downgraded; replays just fill missing charge/card details * cancelled is terminal except for succeeded (money moved wins) * a 'pending' report never reopens a row already recorded 'failed': it is a late delivery (charge_saved_card records 'pending' for a 'processing' intent after its Stripe call returns; the webhook's Checkout 'unpaid' path records 'pending' too) that can land after payment_failed, and reopening would count the decline as money in flight again, blocking manual payments, applying credit, voiding and line edits. A failed row moves on only to succeeded (money moved wins), failed or cancelled. * amount / tip follow the latest pending/failed state until success * a decline does not end an open PaymentSheet attempt: Stripe returns the intent to requires_payment_method and the sheet still on the device can co…

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`
- void_invoice(invoice, reason) — owner/admin. Payments that belong to the invoice's job are detached back to the job, so a replacement invoice (create_invoice_from_job) picks them up again. It is refused while a payment is in flight (payment_in_flight), and when money that is not tied to a job is still on the invoice (refund it first). Money that still arrives for the void invoice later is re-routed by payments_before_write. Locks the job before the invoice (payments take them in that order too).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/InvoiceService.swift`

| done_by | uuid | yes |  | auth user who ticked the item (server-stamped). |
| template_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| template_item_id | text | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id); job_checklist_items_template_item_key (job_id, template_id, template_item_id) WHERE (template_id IS NOT NULL)
- FK `job_checklist_items_done_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (done_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `job_checklist_items_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `job_checklist_items_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `job_checklist_items_template_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, template_id) REFERENCES checklist_templates(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (template_id)
- CHECK: `job_checklist_items_done_by` (done_at IS NOT NULL OR done_by IS NULL); `job_checklist_items_label_check` (char_length(btrim(label)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(label)) <= 200); `job_checklist_items_template_item` (template_id IS NULL OR template_item_id IS NOT NULL); `job_checklist_items_template_item_id_check` (template_item_id IS NULL OR template_item_id ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$')
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `job_checklist_items_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `job_checklist_items_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `job_checklist_items_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)`
  - `job_checklist_items_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)` — CHECK `can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)`

#### `can_read_signature_object`

`can_read_signature_object(p_name text)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`
- May the caller read this signatures object (besides their own uploads)? Managers+: everything of their shop. Other members: the signature of a form or inspection on a job they work. Not a pending upload in a form's public folder <shop_id>/forms/<token>/: its name carries the form token, which would let a technician sign the form as the customer (0023). Once the form is signed the image is readable through signature_path and the token no longer signs anything.
- Called by:
  - RLS policies on: `storage.objects`

#### `claim_storage_purge`

`claim_storage_purge(p_limit integer DEFAULT 500, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `TABLE(request_id bigint, bucket_id text, object_name text)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`
- Worker API (service_role; the storage-purge edge function). claim_storage_purge: up to p_limit object names that are due for removal, oldest request first. A request with nothing left to remove (every object gone or still referenced) is deleted here, which is how requests finish. Requests handed out are leased for 10 minutes (attempts + 1), and locked rows are skipped, so overlapping workers never get the same request.
- Called by:
  - edge:storage-purge: `supabase/functions/storage-purge/index.ts`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`

#### `form_signer_ip`

`form_signer_ip()` → `inet`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0020_field_ops_enums_helpers.sql`
- Client IP of the current PostgREST request, or null when unavailable or malformed. Never raises. Used as audit evidence (form_submissions.signer_ip), so it must not be a value the client chose: 1. cf-connecting-ip — set (overwritten) by the edge network in front of the Supabase API gateway; 2. x-real-ip — set by the API gateway from the peer it accepted; 3. the RIGHT-most x-forwarded-for hop — the one the last trusted proxy appended. Earlier hops (in particular the first) are whatever the client sent and are never used. A header that is present but not a valid address is skipped.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `form_submission_sign`

#### `form_submission_public_json`

`form_submission_public_json(p_submission_id uuid)` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0023_field_ops_forms.sql`
- Curated public view of a submission (no internal ids, notes, IPs or paths beyond the caller's own upload prefix). The customer's name on file is returned only to the signed-in client linked to that customer (null otherwise): a form token can be reached through a booking token that anyone can mint by typing an existing customer's email into the public booking form, so the token alone proves nothing about who is reading.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `public_get_form`, `public_sign_form`

#### `storage_path_uuid`

`storage_path_uuid(p_name text, p_index integer)` → `uuid`

- invoker · sql · immutable · exec: anon, authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0020_field_ops_enums_helpers.sql`
- The uuid in folder position p_index (1-based) of an object name, or null when that segment is not a folder (the last segment is the file name) or is not a canonical uuid. Never raises, so it is safe inside RLS policies. Canonical means the lower-case text form Postgres prints (uuid::text): object names are case-sensitive, and the purge queue (0025) matches the folders of deleted shops, jobs and forms by that exact text, so a folder spelled in upper or mixed case must not be accepted by any storage policy or path validator (its files would never be purged).
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `can_read_signature_object`, `inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge`, `inspection_marks_validate`, `inspections_validate`, `is_signed_evidence`, `job_photos_validate`, `public_form_signature_upload_allowed`, `queue_storage_purge`, `storage_object_in_use`
  - RLS policies on: `storage.objects`
  - CHECK constraints / column defaults of: `storage_purge_requests`

### Trigger functions

- `checklist_templates_normalize()` — invoker · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql` · used by checklist_templates.checklist_templates_20_normalize — Normalize items: generate missing ids, trim labels. Anything that is not the expected shape is left untouched for the CHECK to reject.
- `form_submissions_before_insert()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_10_before_insert — Direct inserts (managers+): everything but the job and template is filled by the server. SECURITY INVOKER: the template and job are read with the caller's own RLS. Missing/foreign templates are left for RLS (which runs before NOT NULL/CHECK), NOT NULL and the composite FKs to reject, so unauthorized callers get 42501.
- `form_submissions_client_guard()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_10_client_guard — Signed submissions are permanent for API callers.
- `form_submissions_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_queue_storage_purge
- `form_templates_normalize()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_templates.form_templates_20_normalize
- `inspection_marks_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_10_client_guard — Direct writes: marks of a signed inspection are locked. The parent is read with the caller's own RLS; a parent the caller cannot see is rejected by the marks policies anyway.
- `inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge — A mark's photo: queued when the mark goes with its inspection (the inspection row is already gone when the cascaded mark deletion runs). A mark deleted on its own keeps its photo (the app removes it through the Storage API, or it stays in the job's gallery); a mark removed with its job or shop is covered by that folder's request.
- `inspection_marks_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_validate — AFTER: the mark photo belongs to the inspection's job folder and exists.
- `inspections_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_10_client_guard — Direct writes: sign/un-sign rules and the signed lock.
- `inspections_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_queue_storage_purge
- `inspections_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_validate — AFTER: the vehicle belongs to the job's customer (or is a vehicle on one of the job's line items); a new signature image exists under the shop folder.
- `job_checklist_items_client_guard()` — invoker · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql` · used by job_checklist_items.job_checklist_items_10_client_guard — Direct (PostgREST) writes. SECURITY INVOKER so trusted code (definer RPCs, triggers, service_role) is not client context.
- `job_line_items_attach_checklists()` — **DEFINER** · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql` · used by job_line_items.job_line_items_attach_checklists — AFTER INSERT / UPDATE OF service_id on job_line_items.
- `job_photos_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by job_photos.job_photos_10_client_guard
- `job_photos_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by job_photos.job_photos_validate
- `jobs_attach_forms()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_attach_forms — Jobs → forms: attach on insert; unsigned forms follow the job's customer.
- `jobs_customer_records_guard()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_customer_records_guard — A job keeps its customer while it carries that customer's signed forms, inspections or invoices (void ones included; jobs_money_guard in 0012 also covers payments). All contexts. Inspections (0022) and form signing lock the job row FOR SHARE, and create_invoice_from_job locks it FOR UPDATE, so none of them can slip in between this check and the move's commit.
- `jobs_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_queue_storage_purge
- `jobs_sync_form_customer()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_sync_form_customer — The previous customer's public upload folder <shop_id>/forms/<old token>/ may already hold a signature image they drew but never submitted. Nothing can reference it any more (the old link is dead and an unsigned form has no signature_path), so it is queued for the storage purge (0025) now; otherwise the customer's image would outlive the form and the job.
- `shop_members_close_time_entries()` — **DEFINER** · `0024_field_ops_time_entries.sql` · used by shop_members.shop_members_50_close_time_entries — Deactivation closes open entries (see the header). AFTER UPDATE OF active on shop_members, every context. An entry whose clock-in lies ahead of the deactivation (a manager set a later time) closes as a zero-length entry.
- `shops_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by shops.shops_queue_storage_purge — Triggers (AFTER DELETE, every context: a row that is really gone). Rows removed by a shop deletion are skipped: the shop's own request covers the whole folder (the cascade runs after the shop row is gone).
- `time_entries_client_guard()` — invoker · `0024_field_ops_time_entries.sql` · used by time_entries.time_entries_10_client_guard

## Communication (0030–0039): templates, messages, automations, campaigns, notifications

### Enums

- `campaign_status`: draft | launched | cancelled  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_channel`: sms | email  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_direction`: outbound | inbound  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_status`: queued | sending | sent | delivered | failed | received | cancelled  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_ — 'cancelled' (beyond SPEC §4.7's list): queued messages withdrawn before sending (cancelled campaigns, recipients who opted out after queueing).
- `message_template_key`: booking_request_received | booking_confirmed | appointment_reminder | on_the_way | job_started | job_completed | quote_sent | invoice_sent | payment_receipt | review_request | follow_up | membership_welcome | invite  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `notification_kind`: new_booking | booking_cancelled | quote_approved | quote_declined | payment_received | inbound_message | form_signed | general  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_

#### `claim_queued_messages`

`claim_queued_messages(p_limit integer DEFAULT 50, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `TABLE(id uuid, shop_id uuid, channel message_channel, to_address text, from_address text, subject text, body text, attempts integer, shop_name text, reply_to text, customer_id uuid, job_id uuid, campaign_id uuid, template_key message_template_key, unsubscribe_token uuid)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`
- Locks up to p_limit due queued messages (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, so concurrent workers never get the same row), marks them 'sending' and returns what the sender needs (unsubscribe_token: marketing email only, for its List-Unsubscribe headers). Due rows that may no longer be sent are settled instead of returned: any comms_withdraw_reason (opted out / suppressed, cancelled campaign, withdrawn marketing consent, cancelled or no-show appointment, stale) → cancelled with that reason; SMS without a shop sending number → failed. Rows stuck in 'sending' for 15 minutes (crashed worker) are failed rather than retried, to never double-send.
- Nullable output columns (`@nullable`): `campaign_id`, `customer_id`, `from_address`, `job_id`, `reply_to`, `subject`, `template_key`, `unsubscribe_token`
- Called by:
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/deliver.ts`

- Which roles may read a notification kind. Every event kind (bookings, cancellations, quotes, payments, inbound texts, signed forms) carries customer names, money or message text that SPEC §3 reserves for owners, admins and managers; only 'general' notices are for every member, so they must never carry such data: anything about money — payments, refunds, card disputes (stripe-webhook) — is sent as 'payment_received'. Checked against the recipient's CURRENT role, so a manager demoted to technician immediately stops seeing (and receiving over Realtime) the manager-only notifications they were sent earlier, and notify_shop_staff never sends a manager-only kind to a technician.

#### `preview_template_message`

`preview_template_message(p_job_id uuid, p_key message_template_key, p_channel message_channel DEFAULT 'sms'::message_channel)` → `TABLE(enabled boolean, to_address text, subject text, body text)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`
- Preview what a template would send for a job (same access rules as enqueue_template_message). Nothing is queued. Links that are not available render blank here, so staff see what is missing (the messaging function's staff send refuses such a message: missing_link); only automatic sending leaves those lines out (comms_render_parts). Lines with a missing optional value (no shop phone, no vehicle …) are left out here too, as they are from every message queued (comms_omit_unavailable_values).
- Nullable output columns (`@nullable`): `subject`, `to_address`
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/messages/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobService.swift`, `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/MessageService.swift`

#### `public_unsubscribe`

`public_unsubscribe(p_token uuid)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: anon, authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0035_comms_campaigns.sql`
- public_unsubscribe (anyone holding an email's unsubscribe link: /u/<token>, token = that marketing email's messages.unsubscribe_token — random, set only on campaign emails and marketing template emails, and never the message id, which staff RPCs such as enqueue_template_message return to technicians). The token is resolved through comms_unsubscribe_tokens (0033), which outlives the message, so the link keeps working after its customer (and the message with them) was deleted. Records the email opt-out of the ADDRESS the email went to (comms_suppress: every customer of the shop with it, now or later, and queued email to it withdrawn) and, while the message exists, of its customer. Returns true when the link was valid.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/campaigns/api.ts`
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/unsubscribe.ts`

`queue_message(p_shop_id uuid, p_customer_id uuid, p_channel message_channel, p_subject text, p_body text, p_job_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_request_nonce text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `messages`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`
- queue_message — staff free-form message to a customer (owner/admin/ manager). Raises when it cannot be delivered (no address, opted out, SMS not configured) so the sender gets feedback. p_request_nonce (see the header): a retry returns the message the first call queued.
- Called by:
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/send.ts`

#### `record_inbound_sms`

`record_inbound_sms(p_to text, p_from text, p_body text, p_provider_id text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `TABLE(message_id uuid, shop_id uuid, customer_id uuid, opt_action text)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`
- record_inbound_sms (service_role; Twilio inbound webhook, signature already verified by the edge function). Routes by the To number to the shop the platform bound it to (shop_sms_numbers — whether or not the shop currently sends from it, so replies and STOPs to a paused number are never lost) and by the From number to the most recently created matching customer of that shop (active customers first). Handles carrier opt-out keywords for the NUMBER (comms_suppressions + every customer of the shop with it, including customers created with it later): STOP, STOPALL, UNSUBSCRIBE, CANCEL, END, QUIT, OPTOUT, REVOKE → opt out START, UNSTOP, YES → opt back in Unknown senders are stored with customer_id null. Staff (owner/admin/ manager) get an 'inbound_message' notification. Idempotent per provider id (Twilio retries). Returns no row when the To number is bound to no shop.
- Nullable output columns (`@nullable`): `customer_id`, `opt_action`
- Called by:
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/twilio_webhooks.ts`

#### `render_template`

`render_template(p_body text, p_vars jsonb)` → `text`

- invoker · plpgsql · immutable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0032_comms_templates.sql`
- render_template — pure; byte-for-byte equivalent to renderTemplate() in supabase/functions/_shared/templates.ts: * placeholder = "{{", optional spaces/tabs, name [A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*, optional spaces/tabs, "}}"; names are case-sensitive * strings verbatim, numbers in JSON form, booleans true/false * unknown names, JSON null, objects and arrays render as '' * single pass: substituted values are never re-scanned * anything else (e.g. "{{ a b }}", "{x}") is left untouched Never raises: a null body renders null, non-object vars count as {}.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/quotes/shared/api.ts`
  - SQL functions: `comms_render_parts`, `launch_campaign`, `preview_campaign_message`, `preview_document_message`, `preview_template_message`

## Integration & reports (0040–0049): pricing, events, online booking, portal, realtime, reports, search

### Tables

#### `integration_events`

file `0041_integration_events.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Idempotency ledger for cross-domain side effects (0041). Written only by definer code; managers+ may read it.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| event | text | no |  |
| job_id | uuid | yes |  |
| payment_id | uuid | yes |  |
| form_submission_id | uuid | yes |  |
| membership_id | uuid | yes |  |
| subject_id | uuid | yes | GENERATED: COALESCE(job_id, payment_id, form_submission_id, membership_id) |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (event, subject_id); (shop_id, id)
- FK `integration_events_form_submission_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, form_submission_id) REFERENCES form_submissions(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_membership_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, membership_id) REFERENCES memberships(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_payment_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, payment_id) REFERENCES payments(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `integration_events_event_check` (event = ANY (ARRAY['booking_created', 'booking_confirmed', 'on_the_way', 'job_completed', 'payment_succeeded', 'form_signed', 'membership_activated'])); `integration_events_one_subject` (num_nonnulls(job_id, payment_id, form_submission_id, membership_id) = 1); `integration_events_subject_kind` ( CASE event WHEN 'payment_succeeded' THEN payment_id IS NOT NULL WHEN 'form_signed' THEN form_submission_id IS NOT NULL WHEN 'membership_activated' THEN membership_id IS NOT NULL ELSE job_id IS NOT NULL END)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `integration_events_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`

- Reads a booking start (see the header): an ISO-8601 date-time with an offset is that instant; one without an offset is wall-clock time in p_tz. 22023 for anything else, and for local times that do not exist or are ambiguous in p_tz (DST changes). - price_services — staff (owner/admin/manager) pricing preview for building jobs, quotes and invoices. p_vehicle_id (optional) must belong to the customer (the customer defaults to the vehicle's owner) and supplies the category when p_vehicle_category_id is null; vehicle-scoped memberships apply only to that vehicle. #### `price_services_core`

`price_services_core(p_shop_id uuid, p_customer_id uuid, p_vehicle_category_id uuid, p_service_ids uuid[], p_vehicle_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_apply_membership boolean DEFAULT true)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0040_integration_helpers_pricing.sql`
- price_services_core — INTERNAL (no caller checks; used by price_services, create_online_booking and public_validate_coupon). For each service id (de-duplicated, input order kept) the catalog price and duration for the vehicle category (category row, else base row — service_price_for). When p_apply_membership, the customer's ACTIVE memberships apply: shop-wide ones, plus vehicle-scoped ones whose vehicle is p_vehicle_id. A service in any applicable plan's included_service_ids is priced 0 with a note naming the plan; the largest plan discount_bps is returned as the suggested document discount (percent). Callers decide whether a coupon replaces it. Result: { vehicle_category_id, tax_rate_bps, duration_minutes, priced, lines: [{service_id, name, kind, taxable, duration_minutes, catalog_price_cents, unit_price_cents, membership_included, note}], memberships: [{membership_id, plan_id, plan_name, discount_bps, vehicle_id}], suggested_discount_kind ('none'|'percent'), suggested_discount_value, totals: {subtotal_cents, discount_cents, tax_cents, total_cents} | null } priced = every line has a price (a missing catalog price is null and the totals are null). Every service must be an active,…
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `create_online_booking`, `price_services`, `public_validate_coupon`

#### `public_cancel_booking`

`public_cancel_booking(p_token uuid, p_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: anon, authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0042_integration_public_booking.sql`
- public_cancel_booking(token, reason, p_now) — customer self-cancellation. Allowed while the job is requested / scheduled / confirmed and, for a scheduled job, until allow_client_cancel_hours before its start (0 = up to the start time). Owner/admin/manager get a booking_cancelled notification. Deposits are not refunded automatically and a redeemed coupon stays redeemed (the shop decides). Technicians may not cancel jobs (SPEC §4.4): they cannot read job tokens (column privileges below), and a signed-in active member of the job's shop below manager who presents one anyway is refused (42501) unless they are the client linked to the job's customer.
- Called by:

`replace_business_hours(p_shop_id uuid, p_rows jsonb)` → `SETOF business_hours`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0092_integration_crm_ops.sql`
- replace_business_hours — replaces all of a shop's business hours in one statement block (owner/admin). p_rows: a JSON array (at most 50) of {"weekday": 0-6 (0 = Sunday), "opens_at": "HH:MM[:SS]", "closes_at": "HH:MM[:SS]" (24:00 allowed)}; [] = closed all week. Overlapping intervals (23P01) or closes_at <= opens_at (23514) fail the whole call, so the previous hours stay. Concurrent saves of one shop are serialized by an advisory lock. Returns the stored rows by weekday, time.
- Called by: no statically named web / iOS / edge-function / SQL caller in this repo

#### `report_revenue_totals`

`report_revenue_totals(p_shop_id uuid, p_from date, p_to date)` → `TABLE(gross_cents bigint, refunds_cents bigint, net_cents bigint, tips_cents bigint, payments_count bigint)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0093_integration_money.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) Cash revenue totals over a date range (shop time zone): gross, refunds, net (excl. tips), tips, count. One row.
- Called by: no statically named web / iOS / edge-function / SQL caller in this repo

#### `set_stripe_refund_total`

`set_stripe_refund_total(p_shop_id uuid, p_payment_intent_id text, p_expected_refunded_cents bigint, p_refunded_cents_total bigint)` → `payments`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0093_integration_money.sql`
- set_stripe_refund_total (service_role; stripe-webhook) — sets a received card payment's cumulative refunded amount to p_refunded_cents_total when it still equals p_expected_refunded_cents (compare-and-set: a concurrent refund event changed it otherwise → 40001, Stripe redelivers). Unlike apply_stripe_refund the total may go DOWN (a refund that failed or was cancelled at Stripe gives the money back to the payment). The status follows (payment_refund_status). Unknown intent / other shop: P0002.
- Called by: no caller in this repo (service-role API / manual operations only)

#### `staff_record_quote_response`

`staff_record_quote_response(p_quote_id uuid, p_action text, p_selected_optional_line_ids uuid[] DEFAULT NULL::uuid[], p_approved_by_name text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_declined_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `quotes`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0093_integration_money.sql`
- staff_record_quote_response — staff record that the customer approved or declined a sent quote (e.g. by phone), atomically with the optional lines the customer chose. Owner/admin/manager. Same rules as the customer's own public_respond_quote: only sent / viewed quotes inside their validity. 'approve' p_selected_optional_line_ids (when not null) must be optional lines of this quote and becomes exactly the selected set; null keeps the current selections. approved_by_name = the name the customer gave (optional, ≤ 200). 'decline' declined_reason (optional, ≤ 1000). Returns the updated quote.
- Called by: no statically named web / iOS / edge-function / SQL caller in this repo

### Trigger functions

- `shop_sms_numbers_log_release()` — **DEFINER** · `0093_integration_money.sql` · used by shop_sms_numbers.shop_sms_numbers_log_release
- `shops_remember_name_on_delete()` — invoker · `0093_integration_money.sql` · used by shops.shops_90_remember_name_on_delete — The shop's name while it is being deleted (the cascade that removes its numbers no longer sees the shop row): remembered for this transaction.

## Storage (buckets and `storage.objects` policies)

| bucket | public | size limit | allowed mime types |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job-photos` | no | 20971520 B (20 MiB) | image/jpeg, image/png, image/webp, image/heic, image/heif |
| `shop-assets` | yes | 5242880 B (5 MiB) | image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp |
| `signatures` | no | 2097152 B (2 MiB) | image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp |

### Object path conventions

Object names are `/`-separated folders. The layout is the one documented next to the bucket definition; the folder checks are derived from the `storage.objects` policies (`storage_path_uuid(name, N)` passed to a public function names the argument that folder N must satisfy); the columns are the table columns that store object names of the bucket (from their column comments or the trigger that requires the object).

#### `job-photos`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/<job_id>/<file>` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `can_work_job`, `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Folder 2: `job_id` of `can_work_job`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`, `is_signed_evidence`
- Stored in: `inspection_marks.photo_path`, `job_photos.storage_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `shop-assets`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/...` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `is_shop_admin`, `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`
- Stored in: `services.image_path`, `shops.logo_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `signatures`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/...` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Whole-name checks: `can_read_signature_object`, `is_safe_storage_path`, `is_signed_evidence`, `public_form_signature_upload_allowed`
- Stored in: `form_submissions.signature_path`, `inspections.customer_signature_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

Other object-path columns (no bucket named by the schema): `profiles.avatar_path`

storage.objects policies:
- `field_ops_job_photos_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_insert_public_form` INSERT to anon,authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND public_form_signature_upload_allowed(name))`
- `field_ops_signatures_insert_staff` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))`
- `field_ops_signatures_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND (can_read_signature_object(name) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
